# TODO — Pipeline

deploy.sh + pre/post-deploy checks, flake checks + eval-time audits, CI workflows, pre-commit, repo/docs hygiene, AGENTS.md upkeep, tq/queue conventions, auto-commit daemon protocol.

Domain LIBRARY of the [TODO system](../../TODO_LIST.md) — every open item for this domain, any lifecycle. The dispatch QUEUE of agent-actionable (`[ready]`) items is `TODO_LIST.md`; the tq pool harvests only the queue.

House rules (enforced by convention, see AGENTS.md → "TODO System"): file an item under the domain that owns the FIX, not the symptom it mentions; one item = one ask + `**Source:**` pointer — verification narratives go to the status report, never the item; `[x]` rows are pruned to `CHANGELOG.md` at every pass; NO system-state narratives here (AGENTS.md and `docs/services/*` runbooks own state).

Tag legend: `[ready]` agent-actionable · `[blocked:user]` needs sudo/browser/external console/owner hands · `[blocked:push]` needs an upstream push/tag (agents implement, never push) · `[blocked:deploy]` waits on a deploy · `[watch]` time-gated verification · `[decision]` owner question.

## Prioritized

- [ ] [ready] **CI: weekly lint-findings baseline-diff job (catch new signoz-query-lint / gatus-pattern-lint trips in ≤7d instead of at sweep time)** — proposal (2026-10-18, E15 of the tile-logos closure): a weekly workflow that builds `checks.x86_64-linux.signoz-query-lint` + `gatus-pattern-lint` (already fast, pure eval) and diffs their output against a committed baseline file (`docs/baselines/lint-findings.txt`, refreshed deliberately); ANY new line = red with the finding inline. Why a baseline and not just red/green: the two lints are already flake checks (green tree = zero findings today), so plain red/green suffices UNTIL the first justified exemption lands — at that point the baseline records it as reviewed rather than silently re-broken. Cost: one 40-line workflow + weekly schedule; the nightly `negative-tests.yml` already runs the negative-test harness, so this rides an existing cadence. Alternative rejected: extending `nixpkgs-compat.yml` (daily full-eval is the expensive leg; the lint-only job must stay cheap). **Source:** the 2026-10-18 tile-logos closure session §e.3/f.5 (the ai-meter underscore regression sat in the tree ~14h before an on-sight catch; a weekly diff would have caught it next morning) **Note 2026-10-19:** the STORE-side axis of this proposal now exists repo-native — `scripts/metric-sweep.sh` + `CASES=metricnames` sweep every dashboard/alert metric reference against the live CH store (local-only; CI stays out by design). The CI baseline-diff idea remains for the LINT-findings axis (hermetic, no store needed).

- [ ] [ready] **pre-commit nixpkgs fast-guard honesty: distinguish nix-failure (nix unavailable/infra-refusal) from lock regression; then sweep all legs for stderr-swallowed infra refusals** — 2026-10-14: the guard's `nix eval … || echo "unknown"` + `2>/dev/null` turned a nix heavy-gate REFUSAL (freeze-storm protection, active from a prior trip) into a false "nixpkgs flake.lock regression … registry rewrote nixpkgs to a tarball" error on a docs-only amend — the tree's lock was fine (direct JSON read proved `original.type: github`) and the wrong message nearly sent the session lock-hunting; the amend only landed via the documented `NIX_HEAVY_GATE_FORCE=1` escape. Fix: (1) the nixpkgs guard must check the eval's rc — rc≠0 prints the stderr tail and SKIPs the leg (gate refusals are infra state, not repo state), rc=0 keeps the current content check; (2) sweep every pre-commit leg that shells out to nix/gated binaries for the same swallow-then-misdiagnose pattern (infra refusal text is good — hooks that discard it break diagnosability). **Source:** docs/status/2026-10-14_14-53_bank-sync-empty-sca-token-diagnosis-phone-change.md §d.2/§e.1/§e.2

- [x] [ready] **Classify tq verify failures before they burn task attempts** — three shapes need different routing: (1) pre-existing/environmental (gate dead at the PRE-attempt rev, e.g. the 05:29 `/run/binfmt` death) → gate-dead holding state + alert, NOT task failure; (2) deadline-timeout-with-progress (`context deadline exceeded` with exit_code 0 and evaluation advancing — tq fact 6663: repair-recipe attempt 2 killed 30 min after claim, mid-`checks.lsblk-column-names`, under ~50-56% IO PSI) → gate-slow, retry with a warm eval cache, NOT task failure; (3) introduced (fails where the pre-attempt baseline passed) → the ONLY shape that counts against the task. With maxAttempts=3, one more environmental/slow cycle DLQs a DONE task — the repair-recipe item was complete since attempt 1 and burned attempts 2-3 on gate failures alone. **Source:** docs/status/2026-09-25_05-33_task-000001a0d68672297273301d23247ceeae6a.md §e2 + tq facts log (task 000001a0d68672297273301d23247ceeae6a) — DONE 2026-09-28 (task queue, upstream go-taskqueue): `VerifyGateError` classification landed in the executor verify path (`internal/executor/verifygate.go`, wired in `agent.go`/`status.go`; facade aliases in `executor/executor.go` per ADR-0016 parity). On verify failure the executor (1) classifies a deadline-killed gate as `gate-slow` (no baseline probe — a deadline kill carries no gate verdict, requeue); (2) otherwise re-runs the SAME resolved verify command in a throwaway `git worktree` at the PRE-attempt rev (captured before the agent runs): baseline fails too (or the probe itself deadline-times out) → `gate-dead`, baseline passes → plain verify-failed (introduced, counts). Cooperative cancel stays on the existing cancelled path and never triggers a baseline probe. The worker requeues both classes WITHOUT burning an attempt on the escalating preflight backoff ladder (Warn "verify gate failed without judging the task" + the reason rides the requeue fact — the fact stream is the alert surface). Tests: 4 executor classification cases + `TestVerifyGateRequeuesWithoutAttemptBurn` (pending/0 attempts on refusal; completes from the same budget with MaxAttempts=1 after the gate heals). Module gates green (executor/worker/facade/root + facade parity); pre-existing unrelated failures proven at the pre-change base worktree `0d566545`: `TestQuestionChannelScopePinsSecondOpinions` (internal/executor), `TestJournalDriftNoDriftAfterRescue`/`TestJournalDriftSeededDriftAllFields` (cmd/tq). Re-verified 2026-09-28 (re-dispatch, no-op): `VerifyGate` executor tests + `TestVerifyGateRequeuesWithoutAttemptBurn` worker test re-run green at go-taskqueue HEAD `ad5dacdc`; both queue surfaces stay closed.

- [ ] [decision] **Unattended self-firing deploy policy** — the session armed a background PSI-watch that would fire `nix run .#deploy` WITHOUT further supervision when the pressure gate's precondition drained (it gave up unfired; a parallel actor deployed at 15:48 instead). The pattern trades responsiveness for supervision: is a gate-guarded unattended switch acceptable (the switch gate re-checks pressure; deploy.sh is the sanctioned path), must each occurrence be owner-authorized, or is background self-firing of switches banned? Also: an unattended deploy bakes in ALL parallel sessions' commits riding the tree at fire time — batch acceptance is implied by the shared-tree model but is a real consequence. **Source:** docs/status/2026-10-01_02-11_architecture-catalog-golive-prep-continuation-2.md §e/§g (HARVESTED at authoring)

- [x] ~~[ready] **Fix the check-doc-links.sh false-positive that red-locks every markdown-staging commit: a generics invocation written inside single-backtick inline code (bracketed type parameter immediately followed by a parenthesized arg list — the chromedp Evaluate shape) parses as a markdown link with target `expr, opts...`, so the whole-set living-docs leg FAILs on docs/todo/upstream.md:107 since daemon sweep `ad623d59` (2026-10-05 10:19) landed that row**~~ **DONE 2026-10-06 (fire 9, ticket `000001a108f0f4cbe1b98e7b80b100000000`):** scanner-side fix per the lint-FP doctrine — `check_file` strips single-backtick inline-code spans before link matching (one span per pass, not a greedy glob, so real links between two spans survive; fence immunity at span size); selftest extended with the FP shape + a real link between two code spans + a ticked-text link, all green (`SELFTEST: OK (anchors, dots, dedup, fences, inline code)`); whole-tree run rc=0 (`OK: no broken relative links or anchors in living docs`); negative probe loud-fails (planted broken link → rc=1, restored).
